def feasible_for_L(L, csub, asub, h0, diag_c, q_needed):
    x_lo=0; x_hi=1
    zero = asub==0
    if np.any(zero) and csub[zero] > L: return False
    pos = asub>0
    if np.any(pos): x_hi=min(x_hi, np.min((L-csub[pos])/asub[pos]) + h0)
    neg = asub<0
    if np.any(neg): x_lo=max(x_lo, np.max((L-csub[neg])/asub[neg]) + h0)
    if x_lo > x_hi + tol: False
    if diag: check forbidden:
       Cq=L - diag_c + h0 - h0*h0
       if Cq < -tol: False
       elif Cq < 0.25 - tol:
          r1=(1-sqrt(1-4Cq))/2; r2=(1+sqrt)/2
          # If [x_lo,x_hi] entirely inside [r1,r2]: infeasible

          if x_lo >= r1 - tol and x_hi <= r2 + tol: False
    return True

def choose_orientation(n_test=64):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(0)
    h = project_feasible(rng.rand(n_test), n_test)
    actual = np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full')
    best = None; best_err=1
    for flip in (0,1):
      h2=h.copy(); c2=actual.copy(); if flip: c2=c2[::-1]
      # do 20 random updates
      for _ in range(10):
        p=rng.randint(n_test); h0=h2[p]
        x=clip(h0 + rng.uniform(-0.2,0.2),0,1)
        # build a using precompute? For small, use generic build_a
        ms,a,diag = build_coord_indices(n_test,p)
        delta=x-h0; c2[ms]+=delta*a; if diag: c2[ms[diag]]-=delta*delta; h2[p]=x
      h3 = h2  # after all updates
      actual2=np.correlate(h3,1-h3,'full')
      c_work2=actual2 if not flip else actual2[::-1]
      # But c2 might have accumulated errors only from formula; compare
      err=np.max(np.abs(c2-c_work2))
      choose min
    return flip

def choose_x_after_L(L, h0, x_lo, x_hi, c_diag, has_diag):
    x_lo = max(0.0, min(1.0, x_lo)); x_hi = max(0.0, min(1.0, x_hi))
    if x_lo > x_hi + 1e-13: return h0
    if has_diag:
        Cq = L - c_diag + h0 - h0*h0
        if Cq < -1e-12:
            return h0
        if Cq >= 0.25 - 1e-12:
            pass
        else:
            r1=(1-sqrt(max(0,1-4Cq)))/2; r2=(1+sqrt)/2

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=h.astype(float)
    T=n/2
    s=h.sum()
    if abs(s-T)<1e-14: return np.clip(h,0,1)
    lo=float(np.min(h)-1.0); hi=float(np.max(h)+1.0)
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       clipped=np.clip(h-mid,0,1)
       ss=clipped.sum()
       if ss>T: lo=mid? Let's determine: increasing mid decreases clipped sum. If ss > T, need increase mid -> lo=mid. If ss < T, hi=mid.
    return np.clip(h - mid,0,1)
```
This projection is Euclidean and exact within. It may be faster than iterative? 80*n clip operations ~56k per projection, compared previous 500*n ~350k. Good. It changes h more globally but correct projection. However coordinate descent objective has N? The projection is onto feasible set, so after pass h feasible. Good.
But if h sum not far, projection small. Use bisection 60 iterations enough.

We can write:
```
def coordinate_solution(c, h, data, p):
    n=len(h)
    h0=float(h[p])
    item=data[p]
    ms, idx1, oth1, idx2, oth2, diag = item
    a = np.zeros(ms.shape[0], dtype=float)
    if idx1.size:
       a[idx1] += 1.0 - h[oth1]
    if idx2.size:
       a[idx2] -= h[oth2]
    c_sub = c[ms]
    if diag >=0:
       a_sub = a.copy(); a_sub[diag]=0.0
       c_diag = float(c_sub[diag])
       has_diag=True
    else:
       a_sub = a; has_diag=False; c_diag=0.0
    pos = a_sub > 1e-15; neg = a_sub < -1e-15
    c_pos = c_sub[pos] if np.any(pos) else None ...
    # unaffected max
    # Since L=2n-1 small, copy
    tmp = c.copy(); tmp[ms] = -1e300
    L_lo = float(tmp.max()) if L>0 else 0.0
    L_hi = float(c.max())
    # If L_hi <= L_lo? feasible at L_lo maybe? If current max affected but unaffected max equal? L_hi at least.
    for it in range(50):
       L=0.5*(L_lo+L_hi)
       if not coord_feasible(L, ...): L_lo=L
       else: L_hi=L
    L=L_hi
    # compute xlo/xhi with same bounds
    x_lo=0.0; x_hi=1.0
    if any pos: x_hi=min(...)
    if any neg: x_lo=max(...)
    x_lo=max(0, min(1,x_lo)); x_hi=max(0,min(1,x_hi))
    if x_lo > x_hi + 1e-12: return h0
    if has_diag:
       Cq = L - c_diag + h0 - h0*h0
       if Cq < -1e-12: return h0
       if Cq < 0.25 - 1e-12:
           disc = max(0, 1-4*Cq)
           r1=(1-np.sqrt(disc))*0.5; r2=(1+np.sqrt(disc))*0.5
           best= None; best_dist=1e100
           # below
           lo = max(0.0, x_lo); hi = min(x_hi, r1)
           if lo <= hi + 1e-14:
               # feasible below segment [lo, hi] (x(1-x)<=Cq for x<=r1)
               # candidates: clip h0
               cand = min(max(h0, lo), hi)
               if cand >= r1 - 1e-14: cand = np.nextafter(r1, 0.0)
               if cand < lo: cand=lo
               if cand > hi: cand=hi
               if cand >= lo and cand <= hi + 1e-14:
                   d=abs(cand-h0); best=cand; best_dist=d
           # above
           lo2=max(x_lo, r2); hi2=x_hi
           if lo2 <= hi2 + 1e-14:
               cand = min(max(h0, lo2), hi2)
               if cand <= r2 + 1e-14: cand=np.nextafter(r2,1.0)
               if cand < lo2: cand=lo2
               if cand > hi2: cand=hi2
               d=abs(cand-h0)
               if d < best_dist: best=cand
           if best is None: return h0
           x=clamp(best,0,1)
       else:
           x=clamp(h0, x_lo, x_hi) # If h0 not feasible? but no diag restrict. choose midpoint if h0 outside? clamp works.
    else:
       # choose midpoint if interval wide? closest to h0.
       x=clamp(h0, x_lo, x_hi)
    return x
```
But if no diagonal and L < current max, h0 not in interval; clamp h0 to interval gives boundary. If interval [0,1] (L above max) h0 chosen no change. Good.
For diagonal, if Cq>=0.25, choose h0 clipped; if h0 outside choose boundary.

Feasible function:
```
def coord_feasible(L, L_lo? no, h0, has_diag, c_diag, x? Need pass pos/neg arrays)
   x_lo=...; if empty return False

```
def choose_orientation(n_test=64):
  rng=np.random.RandomState(123)
  h=project_feasible(rng.rand(n_test)*1.2, n_test)
  actual=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
  errs=[]
  for flip in (0,1):
     h2=h.copy(); c2=actual.copy()
     if flip: c2=c2[::-1]
     data_t=build_coord_data(n_test)
     for _ in range(15):
        p=rng.randint(n_test)
        h0=h2[p]
        # random x in [0,1], not too close
        x=float(np.clip(h0 + rng.uniform(-0.4,0.4), 0.0,1.0))
        item=data_t[p]
        ms, idx1, oth1, idx2, oth2, diag=item
        a=np.zeros(len(ms)); ...
        delta=x-h0; h2[p]=x
        c2[ms]+=delta*a
        if diag>=0: c2[ms[diag]]-=delta*delta
     actual2=np.correlate(h2,1-h2,'full')
     c2_true=actual2 if not flip else actual2[::-1]
     errs.append(np.max(np.abs(c2-c2_true)))
  return 0 if errs[0]<=errs[1] else 1
```
This assumes c2 updates remain accurate through multiple random x (no projection). Since each coordinate update exact for current h; sequential exact updates should be exact analytically up to floating. Good. If flip chosen orientation, error tiny ~1e-13. def run_cd(h0, n, data, orient, seed, deadline, max_passes=20000):
    h=h0.copy()
    c=get_correlation_work(h,n,orient)
    dx=2.0/n
    best_h=h.copy(); best_csum=float(c.max())
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    no_improve=0
    pass_no=0
    fixed_order=np.arange(n)
    while time.time() < deadline and pass_no < max_passes:
       if pass_no < 30:
          order=fixed_order
       else:
          order=rng.permutation(n)
       improved=cd_pass(h,c,data,order,deadline)
       pass_no +=1
       if not improved:
          break
       # To avoid c drift due floating, recompute every pass (already)
       h = project_feasible(h,n)
       c = get_correlation_work(h,n,orient)
       csum = float(c.max())
       if csum < best_csum - 1e-12:
          best_csum=csum; best_h=h.copy(); no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve >= 25:
             # reset to best and maybe continue from there if time
             h=best_h.copy(); c=get_correlation_work(h,n,orient); no_improve=0
             # maybe ready for random restart? if no improvement, break? We'll rely on outer restarts.
             # To avoid infinite reset, break if pass_no large? Let's break after 2 resets.
             # Use a counter resets; if reset more than 3: break
```

Random start generation:
1. `threshold_start(h_best,n)`: Sort h, set top n//2 to 1, rest 0, but if n even sum exact. If n odd? target n/2 maybe .5, cannot all binary. Use fractional: top n/2? n even likely 700. If not, set values 1/0 plus one .5. Function:
```
def binary_threshold_from(h,n):
   idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
   h2=np.zeros(n)
   k=int(n//2)
   h2[idx[:k]]=1
   rem = n/2.0 - k
   if rem>0: h2[idx[k]] = rem
   return h2
```
Then project. But sum exact.

def random_fourier_start(n,rng):
   # generate real periodic-ish? Use random low-frequency sine/cos coefficients
   h=np.full(n,0.5)

       else: h += amp*np.sin(...)

`build_coord_data(n)`:
```
def build_coord_data(n):
    data=[]
    L=2*n-1
    for p in range(n):
        kmin = -max(p, n-1-p)
        kmax = max(p, n-1-p)
        k = np.arange(kmin, kmax+1, dtype=int)
        ms = n-1 + k
        idx1 = np.empty(0,dtype=int); oth1=np.empty(0,dtype=int)
        lo1 = max(kmin, p-(n-1)); hi1 = min(kmax, p)
        if lo1 <= hi1:
            kk = np.arange(lo1, hi1+1, dtype=int)
            idx1 = kk - kmin
            oth1 = p - kk
        lo2 = max(kmin, -p); hi2 = min(kmax, n-1-p)
        if lo2 <= hi2:
            kk = np.arange(lo2, hi2+1, dtype=int)
            idx2 = kk - kmin
            oth2 = p + kk
        else: idx2=...; oth2=...
        diag = int(-kmin) if kmin <= 0 <= kmax else -1
        data.append((ms, idx1, oth1, idx2, oth2, diag))
    return data
```
Note: kmin≤0<kmax always because p between 0,n-1, max >? For n=1 special? n likely >1. If n=1, target sum .5, h=[0.5], objective? Compute correlate length1. But not needed maybe. Handle n<2 gracefully: if n==1 return data with kmin=kmax=0. max(p,n-1-p)=0, ms=[0], first lo=max(0,0)=0 hi=0, oth1=0, second same. diag=0. Good.

Need ensure idx arrays dtype int. Empty arrays can be `np.zeros(0, dtype=int)`.

`coord_update(c,h,data,p,new_val)`:
```
def coord_update(c, h, data, p, new_val):
    old = float(h[p])
    delta = new_val - old
    if delta == 0: return
    ms, idx1, oth1, idx2, oth2, diag = data[p]
    a = np.zeros(ms.shape[0], dtype=float)
    if idx1.size:
        a[idx1] += 1.0 - h[oth1]
    if idx2.size:
        a[idx2] -= h[oth2]
    c[ms] += delta * a
    if diag >= 0:
        c[ms[diag]] -= delta * delta
    h[p] = new_val
```
Important: Uses h with old value at p. If h[p] already set, old wrong. Ensure call before setting h[p]. In pair eval and accept, call `coord_update(c_tmp, h_tmp, data, p, x)` while h_tmp[p] old. Good.

`coord_solution`:
Need use data, c, h. We'll implement with coordinate local.
```
def coord_solution(c,h,data,p):
    h0 = float(h[p])
    ms, idx1, oth1, idx2, oth2, diag = data[p]
    a = np.zeros(ms.shape[0], dtype=float)
    if idx1.size: a[idx1] += 1.0 - h[oth1]
    if idx2.size: a[idx2] -= h[oth2]
    c_sub = c[ms]
    tol=1e-12
    if diag >= 0:
        c_diag = float(c_sub[diag])
        a_sub = a.copy(); a_sub[diag] = 0.0
        has_diag=True
    else:
        c_diag=0.0; a_sub=a; has_diag=False
    pos = a_sub > tol
    neg = a_sub < -tol
    const = ~(pos | neg)
    # const max
    tmp = c.copy(); tmp[ms] = -1e300
    L_lo = float(tmp.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if np.any(const):
       cv = float(np.max(c_sub[const]))
       if cv > L_lo: L_lo=cv
    L_hi = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(L_lo): L_lo=0.0
    if L_hi <= L_lo:
        L = L_hi
    else:
        c_pos = c_sub[pos] if np.any(pos) else None
...
```
But `c_pos` should be view; if we create after L changes, ok. In bisection, need arrays. To avoid checking np.any every iteration, create boolean arrays and values:
```
has_pos = np.any(pos); has_neg=np.any(neg)
c_pos = c_sub[pos] if has_pos else None
a_pos = a_sub[pos] if has_pos else None
...
for _ in range(38):
   if L_hi-L_lo < 1e-11: break
   L=0.5*(L_lo+L_hi)
   x_lo=0.0; x_hi=1.0
   if has_pos:
      ub = np.min((L - c_pos)/a_pos) + h0
      if ub < x_hi: x_hi=ub
   if has_neg:
      lb = np.max((L - c_neg)/a_neg) + h0
      if lb > x_lo: x_lo=lb
   feasible = x_lo <= x_hi + 1e-12
   if feasible and has_diag:
      Cq = L - c_diag + h0 - h0*h0
      if Cq < -1e-11: feasible=False
      elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-12:
          disc = 1.0 - 4.0*Cq
          if disc < 0: disc=0
          r1 = 0.5*(1.0-np.sqrt(disc)); r2=0.5*(1.0+np.sqrt(disc))
          # infeasible if [x_lo,x_hi] lies inside [r1,r2]
          if x_lo >= r1 - 1e-12 and x_hi <= r2 + 1e-12:
              feasible=False
   if feasible: L_hi=L
   else: L_lo=L
L=L_hi
```
But if L_hi-L_lo initially zero? okay.

   Cq = L - c_diag + h0 - h0*h0

   if Cq < 0.25 - 1e-10:
      roots...

`optimize` loop details with pair:
```
def optimize(h0,n,data,orient,seed,deadline,max_passes=500):
    h = h0.copy()
    c = get_corr_work(h,n,orient)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_csum = float(c.max())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    fixed_order = np.arange(n)
    pass_count=0; no_impr=0; resets=0
    dx=2.0/n
    while time.time() < deadline and pass_count < max_passes:
        if pass_count < 20:
            order = fixed_order
        else:
            order = rng.permutation(n)
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.time()+30?) # no, cd_pass will check each coord; no timed sub.
        improved = cd_pass(h,c,data,order,deadline)
        pass_count += 1
        if not improved:
            break
        # pair moves
        if time.time() < deadline - 5:
            h,c = pair_local_search(h,c,data,len(h),deadline,rng,attempts=80)
        h = project_feasible(h,n)
        c = get_corr_work(h,n,orient)
        csum = float(c.max())
        if csum < best_csum - 1e-10:
            best_csum = csum; best_h = h.copy(); no_impr=0
        else:
            no_impr += 1
            if no_impr >= 20:
                resets +=1
                if resets > 4: break

`pair_local_search` implementation:
```
def pair_local_search(h, c, data, n, deadline, rng, attempts, grid=13):
    best_csum = float(c.max())
    accepted=0
    for _ in range(attempts):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        p = rng.randint(n)
        q = rng.randint(n)
        if p == q: continue
        hp = float(h[p]); hq = float(h[q]); s=hp+hq
        lo = max(0.0, s-1.0); hi = min(1.0, s)
        if hi - lo <= 1e-12: continue
        # candidates
        if hi - lo < 10*...:
           xs = np.linspace(lo, hi, grid)
        else:
           # maybe focus around hp: combine global and local
           xs = np.unique(np.concatenate((np.linspace(lo,hi,grid//2+1), np.linspace(max(lo,hp-0.1*len), min(hi,hp+0.1*len), grid//2+1))))
        best_x=hp; best_local=best_csum
        for x in xs:
            if abs(x-hp) < 1e-12: continue
            x=float(np.clip(x,lo,hi))
            c_tmp = c.copy()
            h_tmp = h.copy()
            # update p
            # use coord_update but it copies a arrays; expensive. We'll call.
            coord_update(c_tmp, h_tmp, data, p, x)
            # Now h_tmp[p]=x, q old
            q_new = s - x
            coord_update(c_tmp, h_tmp, data, q, q_new)
            cm = float(c_tmp.max())
            if cm < best_local - 1e-12:
                best_local=cm; best_x=x
        if best_x != hp and best_local < best_csum - 1e-12:
            h_tmp = h.copy(); c_tmp = c.copy()
            coord_update(c_tmp, h_tmp, data, p, best_x)
            coord_update(c_tmp, h_tmp, data, q, s-best_x)
            h = h_tmp; c = c_tmp
            best_csum = float(c.max())
            accepted+=1
            if accepted % 20 ==0:
                # recompute to correct drift
                c = get_corr_work(h, n, orient? pair function doesn't know orient)
```

What about interaction p and q in lags where both variables appear in same term not via q's other? In bilinear expansion sequential works: c = constant + p*x + q*y + p*q*... Updating p accounts terms linear in p and p^2. Updating q accounts terms linear in q (with p fixed) and q^2; cross p*q term is included in q update linear coefficient because p fixed. So exact.

def make_binary_start(h,n):
   h2=np.zeros(n); idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]; k=n//2; h2[idx[:k]]=1.0; rem=n/2.0-k; if rem>0: h2[idx[k]]=rem; return h2

`interpolate_to(h_old,n_old,n_new)`:
```
def interpolate_to(h,n_old,n_new):
    if n_new==n_old: return h.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old); x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new,x_old,h), n_new)
```
No long literals.

Let's verify project_feasible bisection details:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    target=n/2.0
    if n==0: return h
    s=h.sum()
    if abs(s-target) < 1e-13:
        return np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    lo=float(np.min(h)) - 1.0
    hi=float(np.max(h)) + 1.0
    # We need find shift t such that sum clip(h-t)=target. t between min-1 and max+1.
    for _ in range(70):
       t=0.5*(lo+hi)
       ss=np.clip(h-t,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if ss > target:
           lo=t  # need increase t
       else:
           hi=t
    hp=np.clip(h-t,0.0,1.0)
    # fix residual
    diff=target-hp.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-hp
            i=int(np.argmax(room))
            hp[i]=min(1.0, hp[i]+diff)
        else:
            i=int(np.argmax(hp))
            hp[i]=max(0.0, hp[i]+diff) # diff negative
    return np.clip(hp,0.0,1.0)
```
If all room zero and diff>0 impossible, but target ≤n. okay. If hp[i]+diff >1 due diff small, clip.
Residual after 70 bisection maybe 1e-15; fix.

Could projection with t bisection produce h sum exact but not minimal? okay.

`compute_c5` uses `np.correlate` no flip. For n maybe 0? no.
```
def compute_c5(h,n):
    if n==0: return 0.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')) * (2.0/n))
```

`choose_orientation`:
```
def choose_orientation():
    a=np.array([1.0,0.0],dtype=float)
    b=np.array([0.0,1.0],dtype=float)
    c=np.correlate(a,b,mode='full')
    if c.size <3: return 0
    return 0 if c[0] >= c[-1] else 1
```
If c[0] and c[-1] equal? use 0. This should be reliable.

```
def pair_local_search(h,c,data,n,orient,deadline,rng,attempts,grid=11):
    best_sum = float(c.max())
    accepted=0
    for _ in range(attempts):
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n))
       if p==q: continue
       hp=float(h[p]); hq=float(h[q]); s=hp+hq
       lo=max(0.0, s-1.0); hi=min(1.0, s)
       if hi-lo < 1e-10: continue
       # candidate points
       xs = np.linspace(lo, hi, grid)
       # add hp if not included
       # Maybe include local around hp: if grid even? Simpler unique with local
       # To keep vector small:
       if len(xs) < grid+3:
          xs = np.concatenate((xs, [hp, lo, hi]))
       # Evaluate
       best_x=hp; best_local=best_sum
       for x in xs:
          x=float(np.clip(x,lo,hi))
          if abs(x-hp) < 1e-12: continue
          c_tmp=c.copy(); htmp=h.copy()
          coord_update(c_tmp, htmp, data, p, x)
          coord_update(c_tmp, htmp, data, q, s-x)
          cm=float(c_tmp.max())
          if cm < best_local - 1e-13:
             best_local=cm; best_x=x
       if best_x != hp and best_local < best_sum - 1e-12:
          hnew=h.copy(); cnew=c.copy()
          coord_update(cnew, hnew, data, p, best_x)
          coord_update(cnew, hnew, data, q, s-best_x)
          h=hnew; c=cnew
          best_sum=float(c.max())
          accepted +=1
          if accepted % 25 == 0:
             c=get_corr_work(h,n,orient)
             best_sum=float(c.max())
    return h,c
```

```
def cd_pass(h,c,data,order,deadline):
   improved=False
   for p in order:
      if time.time() >= deadline: break
      x=coord_solution(c,h,data,p)
      old=float(h[p])
      if abs(x-old)>1e-12:
          coord_update(c,h,data,p,x)
          improved=True
   return improved
```
Note `coord_update` recomputes a; coord_solution already computed a but discards. Duplicate work! We can modify coord_solution to return a and diag? Or make a shared function to compute a and use for both solve and update. To save time, `coord_solution` could return `(x, ms,a,diag)` or update c itself? Better: `coord_solution` can compute a and after solving c update using same a, returning new x and maybe update. But it needs c current. We can implement `coordinate_solve_and_update(c,h,data,p)` that computes a once, solves, updates c/h if changed, returns True improved. This avoids duplicate. Let's do that.

Function `coordinate_step(c,h,data,p)`:
- compute a, masks etc.
- solve x.
- if change: c[ms] += delta*a; diag subtract; h[p]=x; return True else False.
This replaces coord_solution + coord_update for CD. For pair, we still need `coord_update` that computes a for arbitrary new value. We can keep.

Let's implement `coordinate_step`. It returns bool. It can use local `a` for update. Good.

Pseudo:
```
def coordinate_step(c,h,data,p):
   h0=float(h[p])
   ms, idx1, oth1, idx2, oth2, diag = data[p]
   a = np.zeros(ms.shape[0])
   if idx1.size: a[idx1] += 1.0 - h[oth1]
   if idx2.size: a[idx2] -= h[oth2]
   c_sub = c[ms]
   ... solve using a ...
   x=...
   if abs(x-h0)>1e-12:
      delta=x-h0
      c[ms] += delta*a
      if diag>=0: c[ms[diag]] -= delta*delta
      h[p]=x
      return True
   return False
```
Need if no diagonal, a_sub=a (not copy) for solve; but update uses a. If diagonal, a_sub=a.copy and set diag=0. Good.
If in solve we modify a_sub only, a unaffected.

In bisection, c_pos = c_sub[pos], a_pos = a_sub[pos]. If a_sub is a (no diag), fine. If diag, copy. Good.

Unaffected max: `tmp = c.copy(); tmp[ms] = -1e300; L_lo = float(tmp.max()); if L_lo < 0: L_lo=0.0`. c_sub[const] maybe. Good.

Potential performance: `tmp = c.copy()` for each coordinate; we can avoid by precomputing max of all c and if argmax in ms then compute second? But okay. However in coordinate_step called per coordinate per pass; 700 copies length1400 ~1M floats per pass. Good.

```
def coord_coeff(c? no, h,data,p): returns ms,a,diag
```
This creates a. coordinate_step calls it. coord_update calls it and updates. But coordinate_step also needs c_sub etc. Good.
```
def get_coeff(h,data,p):
    ms,... = data[p]
    a=np.zeros(...)
    ...
    return ms,a,diag
```
Top-level. Use in both. This adds function call overhead but okay.

Exploration with random Fourier starts: Let's implement simple `random_start(n,rng)`:
```
def random_fourier_start(n,rng):
    h=np.full(n,0.5)
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    # harmonics 1..8
    for f in range(1,9):
       amp = 0.6/(f**1.2)
       if rng.rand() < 0.5:
          h += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x/n + rng.uniform(0,6.283185307179586))
       else:
          h += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x/n + rng.uniform(0,6.283185307179586))
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
But pi literal okay math.pi. Use small list of amps? Amp formula. This start smooth, may not beat. Could include one in exploration.

Let's detail `coordinate_step` code:
```
def coordinate_step(c, h, data, p):
    h0 = float(h[p])
    ms, idx1, oth1, idx2, oth2, diag = data[p]
    a = np.zeros(ms.shape[0], dtype=float)
    if idx1.size:
        a[idx1] += 1.0 - h[oth1]
    if idx2.size:
        a[idx2] -= h[oth2]
    c_sub = c[ms]
    tol = 1e-12
    if diag >= 0:
        c_diag = float(c_sub[diag])
        a_sub = a.copy(); a_sub[diag] = 0.0
        has_diag = True
    else:
        c_diag = 0.0; a_sub = a; has_diag = False
    pos = a_sub > tol
    neg = a_sub < -tol
    has_pos = bool(np.any(pos)); has_neg = bool(np.any(neg))
    const = ~(pos | neg)
    tmp = c.copy(); tmp[ms] = -1e300
    L_lo = float(tmp.max())
    if L_lo < 0.0: L_lo = 0.0
    if np.any(const):
        cv = float(np.max(c_sub[const]))
        if cv > L_lo: L_lo = cv
    L_hi = float(c.max())
    if not (L_hi > L_lo):
        L = L_hi
    else:
        c_pos = c_sub[pos] if has_pos else None
        a_pos = a_sub[pos] if has_pos else None
        c_neg = c_sub[neg] if has_neg else None
        a_neg = a_sub[neg] if has_neg else None
        for _ in range(38):
            if L_hi - L_lo <= 1e-11:
                break
            L = 0.5*(L_lo+L_hi)
            x_lo = 0.0; x_hi = 1.0
            if has_pos:
                b = float(np.min((L - c_pos) / a_pos)) + h0
                if b < x_hi: x_hi = b
            if has_neg:
                b = float(np.max((L - c_neg) / a_neg)) + h0
                if b > x_lo: x_lo = b
            feasible = x_lo <= x_hi + 1e-12
            if feasible and has_diag:
                Cq = L - c_diag + h0 - h0*h0
                if Cq < -1e-10:
                    feasible = False
                elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-11:
                    disc = 1.0 - 4.0*Cq
                    if disc < 0.0: disc = 0.0
                    r1 = 0.5*(1.0 - np.sqrt(disc))
                    r2 = 0.5*(1.0 + np.sqrt(disc))
                    if x_lo >= r1 - 1e-11 and x_hi <= r2 + 1e-11:
                        feasible = False
            if feasible:
                L_hi = L
            else:
                L_lo = L
        L = L_hi + 1e-12
        x_lo=0.0; x_hi=1.0
        if has_pos:
            b = float(np.min((L - c_pos) / a_pos)) + h0
            if b < x_hi: x_hi=b
        if has_neg:
            b = float(np.max((L - c_neg) / a_neg)) + h0
            if b > x_lo: x_lo=b
        if x_lo < 0.0: x_lo=0.0
        if x_hi > 1.0: x_hi=1.0
        if x_lo > x_hi + 1e-10:
            return False
        if has_diag:
            Cq = L - c_diag + h0 - h0*h0
            if Cq < -1e-8:
                return False
            if Cq < 0.25 - 1e-10:
                disc = max(0.0, 1.0 - 4.0*Cq)
                r1 = 0.5*(1.0 - np.sqrt(disc)); r2=0.5*(1.0 + np.sqrt(disc))
                best = None; best_d = 1e100
                lo = max(0.0, x_lo); hi = min(x_hi, r1)
                if lo <= hi + 1e-12:
                    cand = min(max(h0, lo), hi)
                    if cand >= r1 - 1e-12:
                        cand = float(np.nextafter(r1, 0.0))
                    if cand < 0.0: cand = 0.0
                    if cand > 1.0: cand = 1.0
                    if lo - 1e-12 <= cand <= hi + 1e-12:
                        best = cand; best_d = abs(cand - h0)
                lo = max(x_lo, r2); hi = x_hi
                if lo <= hi + 1e-12:
                    cand = min(max(h0, lo), hi)
                    if cand <= r2 + 1e-12:
                        cand = float(np.nextafter(r2, 1.0))
                    if cand < 0.0: cand = 0.0
                    if cand > 1.0: cand = 1.0
                    if lo - 1e-12 <= cand <= hi + 1e-12:
                        d = abs(cand - h0)
                        if d < best_d:
                            best = cand; best_d = d
                if best is None:
                    return False
                x = float(min(1.0, max(0.0, best)))
            else:
                x = float(min(max(h0, x_lo), x_hi))
        else:
            x = float(min(max(h0, x_lo), x_hi))
    if abs(x - h0) <= 1e-13:
        return False
    delta = x - h0
    c[ms] += delta * a
    if diag >= 0:
        c[ms[diag]] -= delta * delta
    h[p] = x
    return True
```

if L_hi > L_lo:
  bisection; L=L_hi+...
else: L=L_hi+...
# compute x...
```

def coord_update(c,h,data,p,new_val):
    new_val = float(min(1.0,max(0.0,new_val)))
    old=float(h[p])
    delta=new_val-old
    if abs(delta)<=1e-14: return
    ms, idx1, oth1, idx2, oth2, diag=data[p]
    a=np.zeros(ms.shape[0],dtype=float)
    if idx1.size: a[idx1] += 1.0 - h[oth1]
    if idx2.size: a[idx2] -= h[oth2]
    c[ms] += delta*a
    if diag>=0: c[ms[diag]] -= delta*delta
    h[p]=new_val
```

def optimize(h0,n,data,orient,seed,deadline):
    h=h0.copy(); c=get_corr_work(h,n,orient)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_sum=float(c.max()); dx=2.0/n
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    fixed=np.arange(n)
    cycle=0; no_impr=0; resets=0
    cd_slice=max(5.0, min(60.0, 40000.0/float(max(1,n))))
    pair_slice=max(2.0, min(30.0, 20000.0/float(max(1,n))))
    while time.time() < deadline:
       if resets > 6: break
       time_now=time.time()
       cd_dead=min(deadline, time_now+cd_slice)
       local_pass=0
       while time.time() < cd_dead:
          if local_pass % 5 == 0:
             order=fixed
          else:
             order=rng.permutation(n)
          improved=False
          # Need cd_pass to maybe run multiple? We call coordinate for all p? If we call cd_pass once, may exceed cd_dead; it checks. To avoid a single pass > slice, cd_pass handles deadline.
          improved=cd_pass(h,c,data,order,cd_dead)
          local_pass +=1
          if not improved: break
       if time.time() < deadline - 5:
          pair_dead=min(deadline, time_now+cd_slice+pair_slice) # maybe absolute
          h,c = pair_local_search(h,c,data,n,orient,pair_dead,rng,100000)
       h = project_feasible(h,n)
       c = get_corr_work(h,n,orient)
       new_sum = float(c.max())
       if new_sum < best_sum - 1e-10:
          best_sum = new_sum; best_h = h.copy(); no_impr=0
       else:
          no_impr +=1
          if no_impr >= 5:
             no_impr=0; resets +=1
             h=best_h.copy(); c=get_corr_work(h,n,orient)
       cycle +=1
    return best_h, best_sum*2.0/n
```

Let's now craft final code in final. But before final, run through `coordinate_step` exact code for unbound variables:
```
def coordinate_step(c,h,data,p):
    h0=float(h[p]); ms,...=data[p]
    a=np.zeros(ms.shape[0])
    if idx1.size: a[idx1]+=1-h[oth1]
    if idx2.size: a[idx2]-=h[oth2]
    cs=c[ms]; tol=1e-12
    if diag>=0:
       cd=float(cs[diag]); asb=a.copy(); asb[diag]=0; hd=True
    else:
       cd=0; asb=a; hd=False
    pos=asb>tol; neg=asb<-tol
    anyp=pos.any(); anyn=neg.any()
    zero=(~pos)&(~neg)
    if hd: zero[diag]=False
    tmp=c.copy(); tmp[ms]=INF_NEG; Llo=float(tmp.max())
    if Llo<0: Llo=0
    if zero.any():
       vv=float(np.max(cs[zero])); Llo=max(Llo,vv)
    Lhi=float(c.max())
    cp=cs[pos] if anyp else None; ap=asb[pos] if anyp else None
    cn=cs[neg] if anyn else None; an=asb[neg] if anyn else None
    if Lhi>Llo:
       for _ in range(38):
          if Lhi-Llo<1e-11: break
          L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi); xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0
          if anyp:
             b=float(np.min((L-cp)/ap))+h0
             if b<xhi: xhi=b
          if anyn:
             b=float(np.max((L-cn)/an))+h0
             if b>xlo: xlo=b
          ok=xlo<=xhi+1e-12
          if ok and hd:
             Cq=L-cd+h0-h0*h0
             if Cq<-1e-10: ok=False
             elif Cq<0.25-1e-11:
                disc=max(0,1-4*Cq); r1=0.5*(1-np.sqrt(disc)); r2=0.5*(1+np.sqrt(disc))
                if xlo>=r1-1e-11 and xhi<=r2+1e-11: ok=False
          if ok: Lhi=L
          else: Llo=L
    L=max(Lhi, Llo)+1e-12   # if not bisection L maybe Lhi. If Lhi<Llo due numeric? Use max.
    xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0
    if anyp:
       b=float(np.min((L-cp)/ap))+h0; xhi=min(xhi,b)
    if anyn:
       b=float(np.max((L-cn)/an))+h0; xlo=max(xlo,b)
    xlo=max(0.0,min(1.0,xlo)); xhi=max(0.0,min(1.0,xhi))
    if xlo>xhi+1e-10: return False
    if hd:
       Cq=L-cd+h0-h0*h0
       if Cq<-1e-8: return False
       if Cq<0.25-1e-10:
          disc=max(0,1-4*Cq); r1=0.5*(1-np.sqrt(disc)); r2=0.5*(1+np.sqrt(disc))
          best=None; bd=1e100
          lo=max(0.0,xlo); hi=min(xhi,r1)
          if lo<=hi+1e-12:
             cand=min(max(h0,lo),hi)
             if cand>=r1-1e-12: cand=float(np.nextafter(r1,0.0))
             cand=float(min(1.0,max(0.0,cand)))
             if lo-1e-12<=cand<=hi+1e-12:
                best=cand; bd=abs(cand-h0)
          lo=max(xlo,r2); hi=xhi
          if lo<=hi+1e-12:
             cand=min(max(h0,lo),hi)
             if cand<=r2+1e-12: cand=float(np.nextafter(r2,1.0))
             cand=float(min(1.0,max(0.0,cand)))
             if lo-1e-12<=cand<=hi+1e-12:
                d=abs(cand-h0)
                if d<bd: best=cand
          if best is None: return False
          x=float(best)
       else:
          x=float(min(max(h0,xlo),xhi))
    else:
       x=float(min(max(h0,xlo),xhi))
    if abs(x-h0)<=1e-13: return False
    dlt=x-h0
    c[ms]+=dlt*a
    if diag>=0: c[ms[diag]]-=dlt*dlt
    h[p]=x
    return True
```
Use `max(0,1-4*Cq)` not max(0.0,..). good.

One bug: If bisection not run (Lhi<=Llo), L=max(Lhi,Llo)+1e-12. But if Llo > Lhi due numeric? current h feasible at Lhi, so Llo should <=Lhi. But if not, L = Llo+eps may be infeasible? But final interval may be empty -> return false. okay.

In bisection feasibility, if Cq is negative but x interval empty already, false. Good.

`zero.any()` after `zero[diag]=False`; if zero was scalar? It's array. fine.

Now `cd_pass`:
```
def cd_pass(h,c,data,order,deadline):
    im=False
    for p in order:
        if time.time()>=deadline: break
        if coordinate_step(c,h,data,p): im=True
    return im
```

Pair local search compact:
```
def pair_search(h,c,data,n,orient,deadline,rng,grid=9):
    best=float(c.max())
    acc=0
    for _ in range(1000000):
        if time.time()>=deadline: break
        p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n))
        if p==q: continue
        hp=float(h[p]); s=hp+float(h[q]); lo=max(0.0,s-1.0); hi=min(1.0,s)
        if hi-lo<1e-10: continue
        span=hi-lo
        xs=np.concatenate((np.linspace(lo,hi,grid), hp+span*PAIR_OFF))
        xs=xs[(xs>=lo-1e-12)&(xs<=hi+1e-12)]
        bx=hp; bl=best
        for x in xs:
            x=float(np.clip(x,lo,hi))
            if abs(x-hp)<1e-12: continue
            ct=c.copy(); ht=h.copy()
            coord_update(ct,ht,data,p,x)
            coord_update(ct,ht,data,q,s-x)
            v=float(ct.max())
            if v<bl-1e-12: bl=v; bx=x
        if bx!=hp and bl<best-1e-11:
            ht=h.copy(); ct=c.copy()
            coord_update(ct,ht,data,p,bx)
            coord_update(ct,ht,data,q,s-bx)
            h=ht; c=ct; best=float(c.max()); acc+=1
            if acc%25==0:
                c=get_corr_work(h,n,orient); best=float(c.max())
    return h,c
```
Potential issue: `best` global for pair, but if candidate `v` lower than `bl` (initial current best) but `bl` may be lower than current global due previous accepted? We update best after accept. At start of each pair, `bl=best`. Good. If current global best maybe from recompute. okay.

def optimize(h0,n,data,orient,seed,deadline):
    h=h0.copy(); c=get_corr_work(h,n,orient)
    bh=h.copy(); bs=float(c.max()); rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    fix=np.arange(n); cd_s=max(5.0,min(60.0,40000.0/max(1,n))); pr_s=max(2.0,min(30.0,20000.0/max(1,n)))
    no=0; resets=0; improved_cycle=False
    while time.time()<deadline and resets<6:
       improved_cycle=False
       sub=min(deadline,time.time()+cd_s); pn=0
       while time.time()<sub:

             bs=v; bh=h.copy(); no=0; improved_cycle=True

       if time.time()<deadline-5:

```python
import numpy as np
import time

INF_NEG = -1e300
PAIR_OFF = np.array([-0.25, -0.1, 0.0, 0.1, 0.25])


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if n <= 0:
        return h
    target = n / 2.0
    if abs(h.sum() - target) < 1e-13:
        return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    lo = float(np.min(h)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(h)) + 1.0
    t = 0.0
    for _ in range(70):
        t = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(h - t, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > target:
            lo = t
        else:
            hi = t
    out = np.clip(h - t, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - out.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0:
            i = int(np.argmax(1.0 - out))
            out[i] = min(1.0, out[i] + diff)
        else:
            i = int(np.argmax(out))
            out[i] = max(0.0, out[i] + diff)
    return np.clip(out, 0.0, 1.0)


def compute_c5(h, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / n))


def get_corr_work(h, n, orient):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    if orient:
        c = c[::-1]
    return c


def choose_orientation():
    c = np.correlate(np.array([1.0, 0.0]), np.array([0.0, 1.0]), mode="full")
    return 0 if (c.size >= 3 and c[0] >= c[-1]) else 1


def build_coord_data(n):
    data = []
    for p in range(n):
        kmax = max(p, n - 1 - p)
        kmin = -kmax
        k = np.arange(kmin, kmax + 1, dtype=int)
        ms = (n - 1) + k
        lo1 = max(kmin, p - (n - 1))
        hi1 = min(kmax, p)
        if lo1 <= hi1:
            kk = np.arange(lo1, hi1 + 1, dtype=int)
            idx1 = kk - kmin
            oth1 = p - kk
        else:
            idx1 = np.zeros(0, dtype=int)
            oth1 = np.zeros(0, dtype=int)
        lo2 = max(kmin, -p)
        hi2 = min(kmax, n - 1 - p)
        if lo2 <= hi2:
            kk = np.arange(lo2, hi2 + 1, dtype=int)
            idx2 = kk - kmin
            oth2 = p + kk
        else:
            idx2 = np.zeros(0, dtype=int)
            oth2 = np.zeros(0, dtype=int)
        diag = int(-kmin) if kmin <= 0 <= kmax else -1
        data.append((ms, idx1, oth1, idx2, oth2, diag))
    return data


def coord_update(c, h, data, p, new_val):
    new_val = float(min(1.0, max(0.0, new_val)))
    old = float(h[p])
    dlt = new_val - old
    if abs(dlt) <= 1e-14:
        return
    ms, idx1, oth1, idx2, oth2, diag = data[p]
    a = np.zeros(ms.shape[0], dtype=float)
    if idx1.size:
        a[idx1] += 1.0 - h[oth1]
    if idx2.size:
        a[idx2] -= h[oth2]
    c[ms] += dlt * a
    if diag >= 0:
        c[ms[diag]] -= dlt * dlt
    h[p] = new_val


def coordinate_step(c, h, data, p):
    h0 = float(h[p])
    ms, idx1, oth1, idx2, oth2, diag = data[p]
    a = np.zeros(ms.shape[0], dtype=float)
    if idx1.size:
        a[idx1] += 1.0 - h[oth1]
    if idx2.size:
        a[idx2] -= h[oth2]
    cs = c[ms]
    tol = 1e-12
    if diag >= 0:
        cd = float(cs[diag])
        asb = a.copy()
        asb[diag] = 0.0
        hd = True
    else:
        cd = 0.0
        asb = a
        hd = False
    pos = asb > tol
    neg = asb < -tol
    anyp = bool(pos.any())
    anyn = bool(neg.any())
    zero = (~pos) & (~neg)
    if hd:
        zero[diag] = False
    tmp = c.copy()
    tmp[ms] = INF_NEG
    Llo = float(tmp.max())
    if Llo < 0.0:
        Llo = 0.0
    if zero.any():
        v = float(np.max(cs[zero]))
        if v > Llo:
            Llo = v
    Lhi = float(c.max())
    cp = cs[pos] if anyp else None
    ap = asb[pos] if anyp else None
    cn = cs[neg] if anyn else None
    an = asb[neg] if anyn else None
    if Lhi > Llo:
        for _ in range(38):
            if Lhi - Llo <= 1e-11:
                break
            L = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
            xlo = 0.0
            xhi = 1.0
            if anyp:
                b = float(np.min((L - cp) / ap)) + h0
                if b < xhi:
                    xhi = b
            if anyn:
                b = float(np.max((L - cn) / an)) + h0
                if b > xlo:
                    xlo = b
            ok = xlo <= xhi + 1e-12
            if ok and hd:
                Cq = L - cd + h0 - h0 * h0
                if Cq < -1e-10:
                    ok = False
                elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-11:
                    disc = max(0.0, 1.0 - 4.0 * Cq)
                    r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - np.sqrt(disc))
                    r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(disc))
                    if xlo >= r1 - 1e-11 and xhi <= r2 + 1e-11:
                        ok = False
            if ok:
                Lhi = L
            else:
                Llo = L
    L = max(Lhi, Llo) + 1e-12
    xlo = 0.0
    xhi = 1.0
    if anyp:
        b = float(np.min((L - cp) / ap)) + h0
        if b < xhi:
            xhi = b
    if anyn:
        b = float(np.max((L - cn) / an)) + h0
        if b > xlo:
            xlo = b
    xlo = max(0.0, min(1.0, xlo))
    xhi = max(0.0, min(1.0, xhi))
    if xlo > xhi + 1e-10:
        return False
    if hd:
        Cq = L - cd + h0 - h0 * h0
        if Cq < -1e-8:
            return False
        if Cq < 0.25 - 1e-10:
            disc = max(0.0, 1.0 - 4.0 * Cq)
            r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - np.sqrt(disc))
            r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(disc))
            best = None
            bd = 1e100
            lo = max(0.0, xlo)
            hi = min(xhi, r1)
            if lo <= hi + 1e-12:
                cand = min(max(h0, lo), hi)
                if cand >= r1 - 1e-12:
                    cand = float(np.nextafter(r1, 0.0))
                cand = float(min(1.0, max(0.0, cand)))
                if lo - 1e-12 <= cand <= hi + 1e-12:
                    best = cand
                    bd = abs(cand - h0)
            lo = max(xlo, r2)
            hi = xhi
            if lo <= hi + 1e-12:
                cand = min(max(h0, lo), hi)
                if cand <= r2 + 1e-12:
                    cand = float(np.nextafter(r2, 1.0))
                cand = float(min(1.0, max(0.0, cand)))
                if lo - 1e-12 <= cand <= hi + 1e-12:
                    d = abs(cand - h0)
                    if d < bd:
                        best = cand
            if best is None:
                return False
            x = float(best)
        else:
            x = float(min(max(h0, xlo), xhi))
    else:
        x = float(min(max(h0, xlo), xhi))
    if abs(x - h0) <= 1e-13:
        return False
    dlt = x - h0
    c[ms] += dlt * a
    if diag >= 0:
        c[ms[diag]] -= dlt * dlt
    h[p] = x
    return True


def cd_pass(h, c, data, order, deadline):
    improved = False
    for p in order:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        if coordinate_step(c, h, data, p):
            improved = True
    return improved


def pair_search(h, c, data, n, orient, deadline, rng, grid=9):
    best = float(c.max())
    accepted = 0
    for _ in range(1000000):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        p = int(rng.randint(n))
        q = int(rng.randint(n))
        if p == q or n < 2:
            continue
        hp = float(h[p])
        s = hp + float(h[q])
        lo = max(0.0, s - 1.0)
        hi = min(1.0, s)
        if hi - lo < 1e-10:
            continue
        span = hi - lo
        xs = np.concatenate((np.linspace(lo, hi, grid), hp + span * PAIR_OFF))
        xs = xs[(xs >= lo - 1e-12) & (xs <= hi + 1e-12)]
        bx = hp
        bl = best
        for x in xs:
            x = float(np.clip(x, lo, hi))
            if abs(x - hp) < 1e-12:
                continue
            ct = c.copy()
            ht = h.copy()
            coord_update(ct, ht, data, p, x)
            coord_update(ct, ht, data, q, s - x)
            v = float(ct.max())
            if v < bl - 1e-12:
                bl = v
                bx = x
        if bx != hp and bl < best - 1e-11:
            ht = h.copy()
            ct = c.copy()
            coord_update(ct, ht, data, p, bx)
            coord_update(ct, ht, data, q, s - bx)
            h = ht
            c = ct
            best = float(c.max())
            accepted += 1
            if accepted % 25 == 0:
                c = get_corr_work(h, n, orient)
                best = float(c.max())
    return h, c


def optimize(h0, n, data, orient, seed, deadline):
    h = h0.copy()
    c = get_corr_work(h, n, orient)
    bh = h.copy()
    bs = float(c.max())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    fix = np.arange(n)
    cd_s = max(5.0, min(60.0, 40000.0 / max(1, n)))
    pr_s = max(2.0, min(30.0, 20000.0 / max(1, n)))
    stall = 0
    resets = 0
    while time.time() < deadline and resets < 6:
        cycle_good = False
        sub = min(deadline, time.time() + cd_s)
        pc = 0
        pdead = 0
        while time.time() < sub:
            order = fix if pc % 5 == 0 else rng.permutation(n)
            im = cd_pass(h, c, data, order, sub)
            pc += 1
            if not im:
                break
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
            c = get_corr_work(h, n, orient)
            v = float(c.max())
            if v < bs - 1e-10:
                bs = v
                bh = h.copy()
                pdead = 0
                cycle_good = True
            else:
                pdead += 1
                if pdead > 15:
                    break
        if time.time() < deadline - 5:
            h, c = pair_search(h, c, data, n, orient, min(deadline, time.time() + pr_s), rng)
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
            c = get_corr_work(h, n, orient)
            v = float(c.max())
            if v < bs - 1e-10:
                bs = v
                bh = h.copy()
                pdead = 0
                cycle_good = True
        if cycle_good:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 4:
                stall = 0
                resets += 1
                h = bh.copy()
                c = get_corr_work(h, n, orient)
                bs = float(c.max())
    return bh, float(bs) * (2.0 / n)


def interpolate_to(h, n_old, n_new):
    if n_new == n_old:
        return h.copy()
    if n_old <= 1:
        return np.full(n_new, float(h[0]) if n_old else 0.5)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(xn, xo, h), n_new)


def binary_start(h, n):
    out = np.zeros(n)
    if n == 0:
        return out
    idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    k = n // 2
    out[idx[:k]] = 1.0
    rem = n / 2.0 - float(k)
    if rem > 1e-15 and k < n:
        out[idx[k]] = rem
    return project_feasible(out, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 1100.0
    hard = t0 + max(10.0, total) - 2.0
    orient = choose_orientation()
    try:
        best_h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        best_h = np.full(700, 0.5)
    n_best = int(len(best_h))
    if n_best <= 0:
        best_h = np.full(700, 0.5)
        n_best = 700
    if n_best > 1200:
        best_h = interpolate_to(best_h, n_best, 1200)
        n_best = 1200
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n_best)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n_best)

    if time.time() < hard - 15:
        data = build_coord_data(n_best)
        reserve = min(350.0, max(60.0, 0.30 * total))
        main_dead = t0 + total - reserve - 5.0
        main_dead = max(t0 + 20.0, min(main_dead, hard - 15.0))
        if main_dead > time.time() + 10.0:
            hh, cc = optimize(best_h, n_best, data, orient, seed, main_dead)
            if float(cc) < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hh, float(cc)

        rng = np.random.RandomState(seed + 17)
        for sig in (0.02, 0.05, 0.12):
            if time.time() > hard - 60:
                break
            s = int(min(120, max(25, 0.25 * (hard - time.time()))))
            sub = min(hard - 5.0, time.time() + float(s))
            if sub <= time.time() + 5:
                continue
            hs = project_feasible(best_h + sig * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n_best), n_best)
            hh, cc = optimize(hs, n_best, data, orient, seed + 50 + int(sig * 100), sub)
            if float(cc) < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hh, float(cc)

        if time.time() < hard - 80:
            hs = binary_start(best_h, n_best)
            s = int(min(120, max(25, 0.25 * (hard - time.time()))))
            sub = min(hard - 5.0, time.time() + float(s))
            if sub > time.time() + 5:
                hh, cc = optimize(hs, n_best, data, orient, seed + 900, sub)
                if float(cc) < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = hh, float(cc)

        for factor in (1.15, 0.85):
            if time.time() > hard - 100:
                break
            n2 = int(round(n_best * factor))
            if n2 < 80 or n2 > 1200 or n2 == n_best:
                continue
            hs = interpolate_to(best_h, n_best, n2)
            d2 = build_coord_data(n2)
            s = int(min(180, max(30, 0.45 * (hard - time.time()))))
            sub = min(hard - 5.0, time.time() + float(s))
            if sub <= time.time() + 10:
                break
            hh, cc = optimize(hs, n2, d2, orient, seed + 2000 + n2, sub)
            if float(cc) < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, n_best = hh, float(cc), n2

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n_best)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n_best)
    return best_h, best_c, n_best
```
